    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro)
                          WHERE(Num'ro=FILTER(1..));
        DataItemVarName=DimensionLoop1;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF NOT ShowInternalInfo THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           IF Num'ro = 1 THEN BEGIN
                             IF NOT PostedDocDim1.FIND('-') THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;
                           END ELSE
                             IF NOT Continue THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;

                           CLEAR(DimText);
                           Continue := FALSE;
                           REPEAT
                             OldDimText := DimText;
                             IF DimText = '' THEN
                               DimText := STRSUBSTNO(
                                 '%1 %2',PostedDocDim1."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim1."Dimension Value Code")
                             ELSE
                               DimText :=
                                 STRSUBSTNO(
                                   '%1, %2 %3',DimText,
                                   PostedDocDim1."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim1."Dimension Value Code");
                             IF STRLEN(DimText) > MAXSTRLEN(OldDimText) THEN BEGIN
                               DimText := OldDimText;
                               Continue := TRUE;
                               EXIT;
                             END;
                           UNTIL (PostedDocDim1.NEXT = 0);
                         END;

        DataItemLinkReference=Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr.;
      }
      SECTIONS
      {
      }

                              IF NOT  (recPostReceipLine_G.GET(recPostReceipLine_G."Receipt Type"::Resumption,"Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Collection recept No.",
                                   Line_L)) THEN BEGIN
                                  recPostReceipLine_G.INIT;
                               END;
                             END; // Ajout
                           // <-- 20150512-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-KRE-12
                           //message('%1,%2,%3',Line_L,recPostReceipLinePrestations_G."Receips Line No. Link","Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Collection Recept Line");
                           //<--20141209-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-7
                               IF "Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Net Quantity" = 0 THEN BEGIN
                                 G_Qt'Livr'es:= -"Purch. Cr. Memo Line".Quantity;
                                 G_Qt'brutes:=0;
                               END ELSE BEGIN
                                 G_Qt'Livr'es:=recPostReceipLine_G.Quantity;
                                 G_Qt'brutes := recPostReceipLine_G."Brut Quantity";
                               END;
                               // ESR-7
                               //-->20141219-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-8 Before
                               // IF ((Type =Type::Item) AND ("Line Collection Type" =  "Line Collection Type"::Collection )) THEN BEGIN
                               //<--20141219-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-8 After
                               IF ("Line Collection Type" =  "Line Collection Type"::Collection )  OR
                                 //--> 20220609-Rpt01-ADVv01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESL6
                               ( ("Line Collection Type" =  "Line Collection Type"::Prepay ) AND
                           ("Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Collection Invoice Type" = "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Collection Invoice Type"::"Collection Prepay Invoice"))
                                 //<-- 20220609-Rpt01-ADVv01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESL6

                                THEN BEGIN
                               //<--20141219-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-8 After
                                 // Purch. Inv. Line"."Collection Recept Line"
                                 IF "Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Net Quantity" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                                   TotPoidsNet_G += "Net Quantity";
                                   TotPoidsBrut_G += recPostReceipLine_G."Brut Quantity";
                                 END;
                                   TotMontant_G += "Purch. Cr. Memo Line".Amount;
                               END ELSE BEGIN
                               // ajout pour sommer les regul
                               IF (("Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Collection Invoice Type" =
                                              "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Collection Invoice Type"::Regularization)  AND
                                                       (Type=Type::"G/L Account" )) THEN BEGIN
                                   TotPoidsBrut_G +=Quantity;
                                   TotPoidsNet_G += Quantity;
                                   TotMontant_G += "Line Amount";
                                 END;
                                 //   ESR 20160128 bug MJO fin  Ajout Pour compter les ligne complement dans total
                               IF ("Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Collection Invoice Type" <>
                                "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Collection Invoice Type"::Regularization) THEN BEGIN
                                   IF (("Purch. Cr. Memo Line".Type="Purch. Cr. Memo Line".Type::"G/L Account" )AND
                                     ("Line Collection Type" <>  "Line Collection Type"::Tax) ) OR ( Type=Type::Item) THEN BEGIN
                                      TotMontant_G += "Line Amount";
                                   END;
                               END;

                               END;
                           //  G_Qt'Livr'es:=G_Qt'Livr'es +recPostReceipLine_G.Quantity;
                           //  G_Qt'brutes:= G_Qt'brutes +recPostReceipLine_G."Brut Quantity";
                           //  END; ENlev' pour bon reprise
                           //<--20140604 ESR PB Qte Brute
                             // ESR-5
                           // Avant  ESR 20160128 bug MJO
                           {  IF(Bonprec_G <> "Collection recept No." ) OR ( Ligprec_G <> "Collection Recept Line" ) THEN BEGIN
                               BoolMemeBonLigne_G  :=FALSE;
                               Bonprec_G  :=  "Collection recept No." ;
                               Ligprec_G := "Collection Recept Line" ;
                             END ELSE BEGIN
                               BoolMemeBonLigne_G  :=TRUE;
                             END;
                             // ESR-5
                           }
                           //Apres ESR 20160128 bug MJO
                             IF(Bonprec_G <> "Collection recept No." ) OR ( Ligprec_G <> Line_L ) THEN BEGIN
                               BoolMemeBonLigne_G  :=FALSE;
                               Bonprec_G  :=  "Collection recept No." ;
                               Ligprec_G := Line_L ;
                             END ELSE BEGIN
                               BoolMemeBonLigne_G  :=TRUE;
                             END;

                           // --> 20140226-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-2
                             TablePostWeighingWeighbridge_G.RESET;
                             IF NOT TablePostWeighingWeighbridge_G.GET(CollectReceipts_G."Weighing No.")THEN BEGIN
                               CLEAR(TablePostWeighingWeighbridge_G);
                             END;
                           //<--20140226-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-2
                           // ESR 20160128 bug MJO
                           // Ajout "IF" pour ne pas prendre les analyses si pas typ' collecte
                            IF ( "Line Collection Type"="Line Collection Type"::Collection )
                           //-->20160519-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-12
                               OR  (("Line Collection Type"="Line Collection Type"::Prepay ) AND
                           ("Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Collection Invoice Type"= "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Collection Invoice Type"::"Collection Prepay Invoice"))

                           //<--20160519-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-12
                            THEN BEGIN
                           //fin ESR 20160128 bug MJO
                               //R'sultats d'analyse
                               G_recPostAnalyse.RESET;
                               IF "Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Net Quantity" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                                   G_recPostAnalyse.SETCURRENTKEY("Receipt Type", "Receipt No.", "Sort Order", "Analysis Code");
                                 //  G_recPostAnalyse.SETFILTER("Receipt Type",'= %1|%2',G_recPostAnalyse."Receipt Type"::Receipt,
                                 //                                                      G_recPostAnalyse."Receipt Type"::Resumption);
                                   G_recPostAnalyse.SETRANGE("Receipt Type",G_recPostAnalyse."Receipt Type"::Receipt);
                                   G_recPostAnalyse.SETRANGE("Receipt No.","Collection recept No.");
                                   IF G_recPostAnalyse.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
                                     REPEAT
                                       // Variation
                                       L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.RESET;
                                       L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.SETRANGE("Document Type",L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Document Type"::"Posted Credit Memo");
                                       L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.SETRANGE("Document No.","Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Document No.");
                                       L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.SETRANGE("Purch Line No.","Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Line No.");
                                       L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.SETRANGE("Analysis Code", G_recPostAnalyse."Analysis Code");
                                       IF NOT L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                                         CLEAR(L_recPurchLineVariatPrice);
                                       END;
                                       IF ((G_recPostAnalyse.Value <> 0) OR (L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Unit Price Variation" <> 0)) THEN BEGIN
                                         AnalyseNb_G += 1;
                                         AnalyseResul_G[AnalyseNb_G] := G_recPostAnalyse.Value;
                                         AnaLyseCode_G[AnalyseNb_G] := COPYSTR(G_recPostAnalyse."Analysis Code", 1, 3);
                                         AnaLyseVariat_G[AnalyseNb_G] := L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Unit Price Variation";
                                       END;
                                       // Si moyenne
                                       IF G_recPostAnalyse."Averaged Value" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                                         AnalyseNb_G += 1;
                                         AnalyseResul_G[AnalyseNb_G] := G_recPostAnalyse."Averaged Value";
                                         AnaLyseCode_G[AnalyseNb_G] := 'MOY';
                                       END;
                                     UNTIL (G_recPostAnalyse.NEXT=0) OR (AnalyseNb_G > ARRAYLEN(AnaLyseCode_G)-1);
                                   END;
                                END;
                              END;
                             //PRESTATIONS\\
                             G_MttEcheance:=0;
                             G_MttEcheanceUNit:=0;

                             G_MttTransport:=0;
                             G_MttTransportUNit:=0;
                             G_MttDisinsect:=0;
                             G_MttDisinsectUnit:=0;
                             G_MttSechage:=0;
                             G_MttSechageUnit:=0;
                             G_MttStockFixe:=0;

                             G_MttStockVariable:=0;

                             G_MttAdmin:=0;
                             G_MttAdminUnit:=0;
                             G_MttImpactLog :=0;
                             G_MttImpactLogUNit :=0;

                             //D'sinsectisation
                             G_recLivBord.RESET;
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Collection recept No.","Collection recept No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Line Collection Type",G_recLivBord."Line Collection Type"::Disinsection);
                             IF G_recLivBord.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                               G_MttDisinsect:=G_recLivBord.Amount;
                               G_MttDisinsectUnit:=G_recLivBord."Direct Unit Cost";
                             END;


                             //Stock fixe
                             G_recLivBord.RESET;
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Collection recept No.","Collection recept No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Line Collection Type",G_recLivBord."Line Collection Type"::"Fixe Storage");
                             IF G_recLivBord.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                               G_MttStockFixe:=G_recLivBord.Amount;
                             END;

                             //stock variable
                             G_recLivBord.RESET;
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Collection recept No.","Collection recept No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Line Collection Type",G_recLivBord."Line Collection Type"::"Variable Storage");
                             IF G_recLivBord.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                               G_MttStockVariable:=G_recLivBord.Amount;
                             END;

                             //Administratif
                             G_recLivBord.RESET;
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Collection recept No.","Collection recept No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Line Collection Type",G_recLivBord."Line Collection Type"::Administrative);
                           //  G_recLivBord.SETFILTER("Price Type",'<>%1',G_recLivBord."Price Type"::Journal);
                             IF G_recLivBord.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                               G_MttAdmin:=G_recLivBord.Amount;
                               G_MttAdminUnit:=G_recLivBord."Direct Unit Cost";
                             END;

                             //S'chage
                             G_recLivBord.RESET;
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Collection recept No.","Collection recept No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Line Collection Type",G_recLivBord."Line Collection Type"::Drying);
                             IF G_recLivBord.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                               G_MttSechage:=G_recLivBord.Amount;
                               G_MttSechageUnit:=G_recLivBord."Direct Unit Cost";
                             END;

                             //RECHERCHE DES REFACTIONS\\
                             CLEAR(Engt_G);
                             CLEAR(MBM_G);
                             CLEAR(Divers_G);
                             CLEAR(Tracab_G);

                             L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.RESET;
                             L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.SETRANGE("Document Type",L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Document Type"::"Posted Credit Memo");
                             L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.SETRANGE("Document No.", "Document No.");
                             L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.SETRANGE("Purch Line No.", "Line No.");
                             L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.SETRANGE(L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Analysis Code", '');
                             IF  L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.FINDSET THEN
                               REPEAT
                                 CASE L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Variation Type" OF
                                   L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Variation Type"::"Engagement Bonus": BEGIN
                                     Engt_G += L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Unit Price Variation";
                                   END;
                                   L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Variation Type"::Supplement: BEGIN
                                     MBM_G += L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Unit Price Variation";
                                   END;
                                   L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Variation Type"::TraÎabilit': BEGIN
                                     Tracab_G += L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Unit Price Variation";
                                   END;
                                   L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Variation Type"::"Durability Loss": BEGIN        // 20220816-Rpt01-ADV01,01,00_ADVITAM_esl6
                                     Tracab_G += L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Unit Price Variation";
                                   END;
                                   ELSE BEGIN
                                     Divers_G += L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Unit Price Variation";
                                   END;
                                 END;
                               UNTIL L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.NEXT=0;

                           //<--20140304-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-3


                             //Montant Ligne
                           {
                             G_MttLine:="Line Amount"+G_MttTransport+G_MttDisinsect+G_MttSechage+G_MttStockVariable+G_MttAdmin;
                             G_PULine:=0;
                             IF Quantity<>0 THEN BEGIN
                               IF "Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Averaged Quantity"=0 THEN BEGIN
                                 G_PULine:=(G_MttLine/"Purch. Cr. Memo Line".Quantity);
                                 G_PULine:=ROUND(G_PULine,0.01);
                               END ELSE BEGIN
                                 G_PULine:=(G_MttLine/"Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Averaged Quantity");
                                 G_PULine:=ROUND(G_PULine,0.01);
                               END;
                             END;
                           }

                             IF "Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Averaged Quantity"<>0 THEN
                               Quantity:="Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Averaged Quantity";


                             G_MttEcheanceUNitPrint := ROUND(G_MttEcheanceUNit,0.01);
                             G_MttAutreUnitPrint := ROUND(G_MttAutreUnit,0.01);
                             G_MttImpactLogUNitPrint := ROUND(G_MttImpactLogUNit,0.01);
                             G_Qt'Normes:=G_Qt'Normes+Quantity;

                             // Si r'gularisation ==> Initialiser m'mo T125
                             IF (( "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Collection Invoice Type" = "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Collection Invoice Type"::Regularization )
                              OR  (WClassCpt_G = '6'))  THEN       BEGIN
                                Transport_G := "Purch. Cr. Memo Line".Description;
                             END ELSE BEGIN
                               IF TableShipAgent_L.GET(CollectReceipts_G."Shipping Agent Code") THEN BEGIN
                                 Transport_G := TableShipAgent_L.Name;
                               END ELSE BEGIN
                                 CLEAR(Transport_G);
                               END;
                             END;
                           END;

                           IF ISSERVICETIER THEN BEGIN
                             //TotalSubTotal += "Line Amount";
                             //TotalInvoiceDiscountAmount -= "Inv. Discount Amount";

                           // on inclut la classe 6 en ligne ds le bordereau
                             IF (Type = Type::Item)  OR
                               ( ( "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Collection Invoice Type" = "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Collection Invoice Type"::Regularization ) AND
                                  (Type = Type::"G/L Account") OR (WClassCpt_G = '6'))
                             THEN BEGIN
                               TotalAmount_G += Amount;
                               TotalAmountVAT_G += "Amount Including VAT" - Amount;
                               TotalAmountInclVAT_G += "Amount Including VAT";
                            //-->20220223-Rpt01-ADVv01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESL5

                               IF "VAT %" = 5.5 THEN BEGIN
                               TVA_G[1] +=  "Amount Including VAT" - Amount  ;
                               TVAt_G[1] := '5,50 %';
                               END ELSE BEGIN
                                  IF "VAT %" = 10 THEN BEGIN

                               TVA_G[2] +=  "Amount Including VAT" - Amount  ;
                               TVAt_G[2] := '10,00 %';

                                  END ELSE BEGIN
                                     IF "VAT %" = 20 THEN BEGIN

                               TVA_G[3] +=  "Amount Including VAT" - Amount  ;
                               TVAt_G[3] :='20,00 %';

                                     END ELSE BEGIN
                               TVA_G[4] +=  "Amount Including VAT" - Amount  ;
                               TVAt_G[4] := ' ';

                                     END;
                                  END;
                               END;
                               //<--20220223-Rpt01-ADVv01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESL5

                             END;
                             TotalPaymentDiscountOnVAT_G += -("Line Amount" - "Inv. Discount Amount" - "Amount Including VAT");
                           END;
                           //<--CDAL
                         END;

        DataItemLink=Document No.=FIELD(No.);

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=4;
        DataItemTable=Table125;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Document No.,Line No.)
                          WHERE(Line Collection Type=CONST(Tax));
        DataItemVarName=Taxe Line;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        SETRANGE("Taxe Line"."Cereals Taxe Code","CMC Collection Tax".Code);
                        CurrReport.CREATETOTALS("Taxe Line"."Line Amount");
                        IF "CMC Collection Tax"."Mandatory Tax" THEN
                          TaxeOb_G := '*'
                        ELSE
                          TaxeOb_G := '';
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=VAR
                           I_L@1000000000 : Integer;
                           Trouve_L@1000000001 : Integer;
                         BEGIN
                           TotTax_G+="Taxe Line"."Line Amount";
                           //MessageDebug(STRSUBSTNO('Taxe Lb:%1:%2', Description, TaxNb_G));
                           // R'partition par libell' maxi 6.
                           Trouve_L := 0;
                           FOR I_L := 1 TO TaxNb_G DO BEGIN
                             IF ((TaxLib_G[I_L] = Description) OR
                               (TaxLib_G[I_L] = '') AND (TaxMt_G[I_L] = 0)) THEN BEGIN
                               Trouve_L := I_L;
                               I_L := TaxNb_G;
                             END;
                           END;
                           IF Trouve_L = 0 THEN BEGIN
                             IF TaxNb_G < ARRAYLEN(TaxLib_G) THEN BEGIN
                               TaxNb_G += 1;
                             END;
                             Trouve_L := TaxNb_G;
                           END;
                           TaxLib_G[Trouve_L] := Description;
                           TaxQt_G[Trouve_L] += Quantity;
                           TaxMt_G[Trouve_L] += "Line Amount";
                           TaxTx_G[Trouve_L] := "Direct Unit Cost";
                           TaxTvaTx_G[Trouve_L] := "VAT %";
                           TaxTva_G[Trouve_L] += "Amount Including VAT" - "Line Amount";
                           TaxTvaTtc_G[Trouve_L] += "Amount Including VAT";
                         END;

        DataItemLinkReference=Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr.;
        DataItemLink=Document No.=FIELD(No.);
      }

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=4;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro)
                          WHERE(Num'ro=FILTER(1..));
        DataItemVarName=DimensionLoop2;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF NOT ShowInternalInfo THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;

                        PostedDocDim2.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"Purch. Cr. Memo Line");
                        PostedDocDim2.SETRANGE("Document No.","Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Document No.");
                        PostedDocDim2.SETRANGE("Line No.","Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Line No.");
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           IF Num'ro = 1 THEN BEGIN
                             IF NOT PostedDocDim2.FIND('-') THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;
                           END ELSE
                             IF NOT Continue THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;

                           CLEAR(DimText);
                           Continue := FALSE;
                           REPEAT
                             OldDimText := DimText;
                             IF DimText = '' THEN
                               DimText := STRSUBSTNO(
                                 '%1 %2',PostedDocDim2."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim2."Dimension Value Code")
                             ELSE
                               DimText :=
                                 STRSUBSTNO(
                                   '%1, %2 %3',DimText,
                                   PostedDocDim2."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim2."Dimension Value Code");
                             IF STRLEN(DimText) > MAXSTRLEN(OldDimText) THEN BEGIN
                               DimText := OldDimText;
                               Continue := TRUE;
                               EXIT;
                             END;
                           UNTIL (PostedDocDim2.NEXT = 0);
                         END;

      }
      SECTIONS
      {
      }
       }
    { PROPERTIES

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro);
        DataItemVarName=VATCounterLCY;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF (NOT GLSetup."Print VAT specification in LCY") OR
                           ("Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Currency Code"  = '') OR
                           (VATAmountLine.GetTotalVATAmount = 0) THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;

                        SETRANGE(Num'ro,1,VATAmountLine.COUNT);
                        CurrReport.CREATETOTALS(VALVATBaseLCY,VALVATAmountLCY);

                        IF GLSetup."LCY Code" = '' THEN
                          VALSpecLCYHeader := Text007 + Text008
                        ELSE
                          VALSpecLCYHeader := Text007 + FORMAT(GLSetup."LCY Code");

                        CurrExchRate.FindCurrency("Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Posting Date","Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Currency Code",1);
                        CalculatedExchRate := ROUND(1 / "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Currency Factor" * CurrExchRate."Exchange Rate Amount",0.000001);
                        VALExchRate := STRSUBSTNO(Text009,CalculatedExchRate,CurrExchRate."Exchange Rate Amount");
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           VATAmountLine.GetLine(Num'ro);

                           VALVATBaseLCY := ROUND(VATAmountLine."VAT Base" / "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Currency Factor");
                           VALVATAmountLCY := ROUND(VATAmountLine."VAT Amount" / "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Currency Factor");
                         END;

      }

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro)
                          WHERE(Num'ro=CONST(1));
        DataItemVarName=Total;
        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           G_recCompEntr.RESET;
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("SettOff Type",G_recCompEntr."SettOff Type"::Posted);
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Invoice No","Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."No.");
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Entry Type",G_recCompEntr."Entry Type"::Customer);
                           G_recCompEntr.CALCSUMS(Amount);
                           G_MttComp:=G_recCompEntr.Amount;

                           G_recCompEntr.RESET;
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("SettOff Type",G_recCompEntr."SettOff Type"::Posted);
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Invoice No","Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."No.");
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Entry Type",G_recCompEntr."Entry Type"::Vendor);
                           G_recCompEntr.CALCSUMS(Amount);
                           G_MttFourLett:=G_recCompEntr.Amount;

                           G_recCompEntr.RESET;
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("SettOff Type",G_recCompEntr."SettOff Type"::Posted);
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Invoice No","Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."No.");
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Entry Type",G_recCompEntr."Entry Type"::Loan);
                           G_recCompEntr.CALCSUMS(Amount);
                           G_MttDEtte:=G_recCompEntr.Amount;

                           //WSA
                           CalcAmountForEDLC_G := "Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Amount Including VAT"-G_MttComp-G_MttFourLett-G_MttDEtte;
                         END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE DocumentCaption@4() : Text[250];
    BEGIN
      IF "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Prepayment Credit Memo" THEN
        EXIT(Text010);
      EXIT(Text004);
    END;

    PROCEDURE MessageDebug@1000000013(Message_P@1000000000 : Text[250]);
    BEGIN
      IF ((USERID = 'ERIC SAINT-LEGER') OR (USERID = 'CHRISTOPHE DAL')) THEN BEGIN
        IF MessageDebugNb_G < 20 THEN BEGIN
          MESSAGE(Message_P);
          MessageDebugNb_G += 1;
        END;
      END;
    END;

  <Code>Public Function BlankZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value = 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankZeroAndPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNeg(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNegAndZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Shared Data1 as Object
Shared Data2 as Object
Shared Data3 as Object

Public Function GetData(Num as Integer, Group as integer) as Object
if Group = 1 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data1),Chr(177))))
End If

if Group = 2 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data2),Chr(177))))
End If

if Group = 3 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data3),Chr(177))))
End If
End Function

Public Function SetData(NewData as Object,Group as integer)
  If Group = 1 and NewData &gt; "" Then
      Data1 = NewData
  End If

  If Group = 2 and NewData &gt; "" Then
      Data2 = NewData
  End If

  If Group = 3 and NewData &gt; "" Then
      Data3 = NewData
  End If
End Function

Shared PictureData1 as Object
Shared PictureData2 as Object
Shared PictureData3 as Object


Public Function GetPicture(Group as integer) as Object
if Group = 1 then
   Return PictureData1
End If

if Group = 2 then
   Return PictureData2
End If

if Group = 3 then
   Return PictureData3
End If
End Function

Public Function SetPicture(NewData as Object,Group as integer)
  If Group = 1 and NewData &gt; "" Then
      PictureData1 = NewData
  End If

  If Group = 2 and NewData &gt; "" Then
      PictureData2 = NewData
  End If

  If Group = 3 and NewData &gt; "" Then
      PictureData3 = NewData
  End If
End Function

REM Reset Page Number:

Shared offset as Integer
Shared newPage as Object
Shared currentgroup1 as Object
Shared currentgroup2 as Object
Shared currentgroup3 as Object

Public Function GetGroupPageNumber(NewPage as Boolean, pagenumber as Integer) as Object
  If NewPage
    offset = pagenumber - 1
  End If
  Return pagenumber - offset
End Function

Public Function IsNewPage(group1 as Object, group2 as Object, group3 as Object) As Boolean
newPage = FALSE
If Not (group1 = currentgroup1)
    newPage = TRUE
    currentgroup1 = group1
    currentgroup2 = group2
    currentgroup3 = group3
     ELSE 
      If Not (group2 = currentgroup2)
        newPage = TRUE 
        currentgroup2 = group2
        currentgroup3 = group3
        ELSE
          If Not (group3 = currentgroup3)
          newPage = TRUE 
          currentgroup3 = group3
         End If
     End If
  End If
Return newPage
End Function
